## 27 October 2026 — Claude (psyche slice B): social/affective memory traces + memory-ability sliders (schema v10)
Run `main-claude-psyche-1793112663` (v3 main lock). Slice B DONE.

## 27 October 2026 — Claude (psyche slice C): mood course – bipolar spectrum, cycling, persistent low mood (schema v11)

Muted = no mania, off = nothing; player never locked out.

## 29 October 2026 — Czech TTS "natural voice" rework (main-claude-tts2)
- Owner feedback on the first TTS voice: "robotic and creepy"; decision: improve the existing formant synthesis (no neural model, no Simlish). Until the owner approves the new sound, `GameSettings.DEFAULTS.character_voices` = `"wordless"` (AudioManager fallback too); "Řeč z textu" stays selectable. Existing saved settings keep the player's choice.
- Diagnosis (Praat/parselmouth + numpy on the 8 owner samples + 3 extra sentences): muffled spectrum (vowel 2–4 kHz band −36 dB re 0–1 kHz), uniform jitter ≈ 1 % / shimmer ≈ 6 % + period-doubling from roughness 0.35, flat piecewise-linear F0 plateaus (up to 115 ms, hard clamps), box-shaped per-phoneme amplitude, clicks at onsets/joins and formant jumps at plosives, tanh saturation, dry signal.
- `scripts/audio/czech_tts.gd` v2: KLGLOTT88 flow-derivative source with polyBLEP closure, style open quotient and tilt, Gaussian jitter/shimmer (≈ 0.4 % / 2.5 % at default roughness), glottal-pulsed aspiration; Klatt cascade F1–F6 with realistic bandwidths, nasal antiformant + murmur; control tracks smoothed zero-phase (≈ 40 ms formant transitions, loci, undershoot), syllable/phrase gain shaping, VOT aspiration, place-shaped bursts, r taps, ř trill+friction; F0 micro-prosody (drift, intrinsic pitch, consonant perturbation, linear range compression instead of clamps, spread yes/no rise); longer, Czech-like timing and pauses; low-pass, light early reflections, 2:1 compression, −3 dBFS peak. Public API, inputs (text + public voice + style) and determinism unchanged.
- Measured before → after (11 sentences): vowel 2–4 kHz band −36.1 → −22.1 dB; vowel HNR 21.3 → 18.2 dB (sad/tired ≈ 13–14); longest flat F0 run in a vowel 115 → 40 ms; isolated clicks 8 → 0 (16 kHz: 2 → 0); 5 ms level jump p99 20.2 → 8.8 dB; peak −2.0 (saturated) → −3.7 dBFS. Render time ≈ 40–130 ms per sample locally. Nobody could listen; no ASR check (model hosts blocked by the session proxy).
- Tests: `test_tts` 63 → 82 (new naturalness section: clicks, edges, HNR/breathiness, F0 flatness/jumps/spread/ceiling, bandwidths, tilt, segment levels, timing ranges, peak, interleaved determinism, speed); local regressions voice 99, audio tone 222, Audio Engine SFX 164, editor UI 68, host editor 10, playable slice 70, multiplayer 67, character_net 83 — all exit 0. Docs: docs/audio.md (TTS section + rework table), docs/testing.md.
- NEXT: owner listens to the new samples (scratchpad tts_samples_v2, with a matching before/ set); if approved, set the default back to "tts" and flip the test expectation.
- Server (Godot 4.7.2, live project, lock main-claude-tts2-1793255854): 6 files fetched from GitHub raw @ 6fad61e and MD5-verified 6/6 after checking the server base MD5s (CHECKPOINT/DEVLOG entries appended to the server copies, which carry newer entries from other runs); test_tts 82, voice 99, audio tone 222, Audio Engine SFX 164, editor UI 68, playable slice 81 (server version), multiplayer 67, character_net 83 — all exit 0. Server timing: 5.3 s of speech in 279 ms at 22.05 kHz, a 3 s line in 107 ms at the in-game 16 kHz; identical bytes on repeat.
